# ANSYS Fluent Microchannel

Use the user-required chain: **ANSYS Workbench → Geometry → ANSYS Meshing → Fluent**. Do not bypass ANSYS Meshing with Fluent Meshing unless the user explicitly changes this preference.

## Start with a simulation contract

Before solving, record:

- CAD path, ANSYS version, length unit, dimensionality, and whether the CAD contains both fluid and solid volumes.
- Which faces are `inlet`, `outlet`, `heat`, `symmetry`, and external `wall`.
- Fluid and solid materials, inlet specification, inlet temperature, outlet pressure, heat input, and intended outputs.
- Whether the case is a literal reproduction, a geometry-adapted pilot, or a production case.

Never infer physical boundaries solely from screen position. A face is an inlet/outlet only when topology, flow direction, or user confirmation supports it.

## Workflow

1. Create or open a **Fluid Flow (Fluent)** system in Workbench.
2. Import the CAD through the Geometry cell. Confirm units and body count. Repair gaps, overlaps, slivers, non-manifold edges, and missing fluid volume before meshing.
3. Open the Mesh cell in ANSYS Meshing. Confirm the solver preference is Fluent.
4. Prefer sweep/mapped hex mesh where the topology permits it. Add local sizing across channel depth, fins/teeth, inlet/outlet transitions, and solid-fluid interfaces.
5. Create explicit Named Selections. Required minimum: `inlet`, `outle<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="654" />ow (Fluent) system and links a CAD file. Run the journal with `RunWB2.exe -B -R <journal.wbjn>`. The correct Workbench template name is `Fluid Flow`; adding `Solver="Fluent"` to `GetTemplate` is not portable in the tested 2024 R2 installation.

Automation must stop before geometric face assignment unless stable named topology already exists. Record the face-zone map before generating any Fluent journal.

## Evidence and reporting

When using the tutorial method, distinguish:

- values visibly entered in the video;
- values stated in the paper shown in the video;
- values chosen for the user's adapted model.

Report project path, CAD linkage, boundary map, mesh metrics, solver settings, convergence evidence, and any unresolved physical assumption.


---ARCHIVE CONVENTION---

# Microchannel simulation archive convention

The user's microchannel ANSYS/Fluent simulation archive is now rooted at `D:\Codex\Microchannel_Fluent_Archive`. Store future reproducible simulation milestones in sequential folders named `V1_...`, `V2_...`, `V3_...`, and so on. Each version folder must contain a `README.md` describing its purpose, boundary conditions, changes from the prior version, results, file layout, and unresolved limitations. Keep common literature and parameter evidence under `00_References`; do not retain failed launches, wrong-boundary cases, transient video-frame caches, or other disposable intermediates in the archive.

原因是：

- Fluent 是通过外部自动化程序读取网格后独立求解的。
- 得到的 `.cas.h5` 和 `.dat.h5` 是有效且收敛的，但没有回写到 Workbench 的 Fluent 系统。
- 因此截图中：
  - `几何结构`、`网格`显示绿色对勾；
  - `设置 Setup` 显示问号；
  - `解决方案 Solution` 显示问号；
  - `结果 Results` 也没有更新。

这表示“Workbench不知道外部求解已经完成”，并不表示已有 Case/Data 没有收敛。

以后我将采用你截图中的方式：

```text
Geometry
   ↓
ANSYS Meshing
   ├── Fluent：F100_Q600
   ├── Fluent：F100_Q800
   ├── Fluent：F120_Q600
   └── Fluent：F120_Q800
```

同一个几何和网格只建立一次，不同流量、热流密度、入口温度或物性模型分别建立独立 Fluent 分支。每个分支都会使用清楚的工况名称。

同时执行以下规则：

- Fluent 使用3D双精度。
- 当前许可证只支持4核并行，因此默认使用4核。
- 如果以后检测到许可证支持更多核数，再提高进程数。
- 不直接照截图设置18核，因为超过许可证上限可能无法启动，或者被自动限制。
- Fluent 必须从相应 Workbench `Setup` 单元进入。
- 设置和求解完成后保存 Workbench 工程，使 `Setup` 和 `Solution` 显示绿色状态。
- 如果需要完整的 `Results` 绿色状态，则同时更新 Fluent 后处理或 CFD-Post。
- 外部 Case/Data 可以作为备份，但不能代替 Workbench 内部流程状态。

当前 V2 的实际计算结果是完整的，但 Workbench 封装不完整。后续修正时应建立一个新版本，例如：

`V3_50um_sJMC_Workbench_MultiCase`

其中共享同一套几何和网格，并把已有100 ml/min、600 W/cm²算例导入第一个 Fluent 分支，再建立其他热流和流量分支。

---PREFERENCE---

# Workbench multi-case and Fluent launch preferences

For future ANSYS microchannel simulations, use one shared Geometry and ANSYS Meshing upstream in Workbench and branch the mesh into multiple independently named Fluent systems for different flow rates, heat fluxes, or other boundary-condition cases. The saved Workbench project must contain the Fluent setup and solution state, rather than only saving standalone `.cas.h5` and `.dat.h5` files outside Workbench. Launch Fluent in 3D double precision and use the highest effective local process count allowed by the installed license; the currently observed license permits 4-way parallel execution, so use 4 solver processes unless a later live check confirms a higher limit. Ensure Workbench Setup and Solution cells are updated/saved; update Results through Fluent/CFD-Post when a completed Results cell is required.

# encoding: utf-8

# 2024 R2

SetScriptVersion(Version="24.2.133")

template1 = GetTemplate(TemplateName="Fluid Flow")

system1 = template1.CreateSystem()

geometry1 = system1.GetContainer(ComponentName="Geometry")

geometry1.SetFile(FilePath="C:/Users/PC/Desktop/micro channel/channel with manifold/yaner shang/origin jet/50juchi/danyaun/danyuan 50 juchi mmc.STEP")

geometryComponent1 = system1.GetComponent(Name="Geometry")

geometryComponent1.Refresh()

meshComponent1 = system1.GetComponent(Name="Mesh")

meshComponent1.Refresh()

Save(Overwrite=True)

mesh1 = system1.GetContainer(ComponentName="Mesh")

mesh1.Edit()

mesh1.SendCommand(

    Command="""
from System import Environment
from System.IO import File

report_path = 'C:/Users/PC/Documents/Codex/2026-09-11/x/outputs/wu2023_corrected_mesh_report.txt'
lines = []

def add_face_named_selection(name, ids):
    ns = ExtAPI.DataModel.Project.Model.AddNamedSelection()
    ns.Name = name
    sel = ExtAPI.SelectionManager.CreateSelectionInfo(SelectionTypeEnum.GeometryEntities)
    sel.Ids = ids
    ns.Location = sel
    lines.append('{0}={1}'.format(name, list(ids)))

try:
    # Vertical-flow top faces identified from Fig. 2 and the STEP face areas.
    add_face_named_selection('inlet', [603])
    add_face_named_selection('outlet', [630])
    add_face_named_selection('heated_bottom', [329])
    add_face_named_selection('symmetry_side_1', [230, 301, 331, 601, 628])
    add_face_named_selection('symmetry_side_2', [272, 306, 333, 599, 626])

    mesh = ExtAPI.DataModel.Project.Model.Mesh
    mesh.ElementSize = Quantity('0.005 [mm]')
    lines.append('global_element_size=0.005 mm')
    mesh.GenerateMesh()
    lines.append('mesh_generated=True')
    mesh_data = ExtAPI.DataModel.MeshDataByName('Global')
    lines.append('node_count={0}'.format(mesh_data.Nodes.Count))
    lines.append('element_count={0}'.format(mesh_data.Elements.Count))
except Exception as error:
    lines.append('error={0}'.format(error))

File.WriteAllText(report_path, Environment.NewLine.join(lines))
""",

    Language="Python")

mesh1.Exit()

Save(Overwrite=True)

2.9. Connecting Systems in Workbench (https://ansyshelp.ansys.com/public/Views/Secured/corp/v252/en/flu_wb/flu_wb_sec_wb_connect_systems.html)
citeturn17search0 [wordlim: 200] Published: last year; Crawled: last month; A shared data connection is represented in the Project Schematic by a line with a square on its right (target) side Image  (see Figure 2.4: Connected Systems Within Workbench). ... You can connect multiple Mesh cells of Mesh component systems to the Setup Cell of a Fluent-based system.

## 2.9. Connecting Systems in Workbench

In Workbench, you can create connections between multiple systems that enable the systems to access the same data. This is useful, for instance, when you want to compare the results from multiple Fluent-based systems in the same Ansys CFD-Post session. In this case, you would connect the various Solution cells to one Results cell (either in one of your Fluent-based systems or in a separate Results system). When you double-click that Results cell, the results from all connected systems will be loaded into Ansys CFD-Post at the same time.

Another example would be where you want to load multiple meshes created in the Ansys Meshing application to your Fluent-based system. See Connecting Multiple Upstream Meshes to a Setup Cell of a Fluent-Based System for further information on such connections.

Workbench supports two different types of connections:

  * Shared data connections

    * Used when the inputs and outputs of the two connected cells are identical

    * Can only be created between two cells of the same type

A shared data connection is represented in the Project Schematic by a line with a square on its right (target) side Image  (see Figure 2.4: Connected Systems Within Workbench).

  * Transfer data connections

    * Used when the output of one cell is used as the input to the connected cell

    * Usually created between two cells<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="7987" />.ansys.com/public/Views/Secured/corp/v252/en/wb2_js/wb2js_scriptingapps.html)

# ANSYS Fluent Microchannel

Use the user-required chain: **ANSYS Workbench → Geometry → ANSYS Meshing → Fluent**. Do not bypass ANSYS Meshing with Fluent Meshing unless the user explicitly changes this preference.

## Start with a simulation contract

Before solving, record:

- CAD path, ANSYS version, length unit, dimensionality, and whether the CAD contains both fluid and solid volumes.
- Which faces are `inlet`, `outlet`, `heat`, `symmetry`, and external `wall`.
- Fluid and solid materials, inlet specification, inlet temperature, outlet pressure, heat input, and intended outputs.
- Whether the case is a literal reproduction, a geometry-adapted pilot, or a production case.

Never infer physical boundaries solely from screen position. A face is an inlet/outlet only when topology, flow direction, or user confirmation supports it.

## Workflow

1. Create or open a **Fluid Flow (Fluent)** system in Workbench.
2. Import the CAD through the Geometry cell. Confirm units and body count. Repair gaps, overlaps, slivers, non-manifold edges, and missing fluid volume before meshing.
3. Open the Mesh cell in ANSYS Meshing. Confirm the solver preference is Fluent.
4. Prefer sweep/mapped hex mesh where the topology permits it. Add local sizing across channel depth, fins/teeth, inlet/outlet transitions, and solid-fluid interfaces.
5. Create explicit Named Selections. Required minimum: `inlet`, `outle<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="654" />ow (Fluent) system and links a CAD file. Run the journal with `RunWB2.exe -B -R <journal.wbjn>`. The correct Workbench template name is `Fluid Flow`; adding `Solver="Fluent"` to `GetTemplate` is not portable in the tested 2024 R2 installation.

Automation must stop before geometric face assignment unless stable named topology already exists. Record the face-zone map before generating any Fluent journal.

## Evidence and reporting

When using the tutorial method, distinguish:

- values visibly entered in the video;
- values stated in the paper shown in the video;
- values chosen for the user's adapted model.

Report project path, CAD linkage, boundary map, mesh metrics, solver settings, convergence evidence, and any unresolved physical assumption.


---ARCHIVE CONVENTION---

# Microchannel simulation archive convention

The user's microchannel ANSYS/Fluent simulation archive is now rooted at `D:\Codex\Microchannel_Fluent_Archive`. Store future reproducible simulation milestones in sequential folders named `V1_...`, `V2_...`, `V3_...`, and so on. Each version folder must contain a `README.md` describing its purpose, boundary conditions, changes from the prior version, results, file layout, and unresolved limitations. Keep common literature and parameter evidence under `00_References`; do not retain failed launches, wrong-boundary cases, transient video-frame caches, or other disposable intermediates in the archive.

[71] assistant: 是的，你的判断正确。当前 Workbench 文件的 Fluent 流程确实没有完整封装，这是我之前保存方式的问题。

原因是：

- Workbench 中只完成并保存了 Geometry 和 ANSYS Meshing。
- Fluent 是通过外部自动化程序读取网格后独立求解的。
- 得到的 `.cas.h5` 和 `.dat.h5` 是有效且收敛的，但没有回写到 Workbench 的 Fluent 系统。
- 因此截图中：
  - `几何结构`、`网格`显示绿色对勾；
  - `设置 Setup` 显示问号；
  - `解决方案 Solution` 显示问号；
  - `结果 Results` 也没有更新。

这表示“Workbench不知道外部求解已经完成”，并不表示已有 Case/Data 没有收敛。

以后我将采用你截图中的方式：

```text
Geometry
   ↓
ANSYS Meshing
   ├── Fluent：F100_Q600
   ├── Fluent：F100_Q800
   ├── Fluent：F120_Q600
   └── Fluent：F120_Q800
```

同一个几何和网格只建立一次，不同流量、热流密度、入口温度或物性模型分别建立独立 Fluent 分支。每个分支都会使用清楚的工况名称。

同时执行以下规则：

- Fluent 使用3D双精度。
- 当前许可证只支持4核并行，因此默认使用4核。
- 如果以后检测到许可证支持更多核数，再提高进程数。
- 不直接照截图设置18核，因为超过许可证上限可能无法启动，或者被自动限制。
- Fluent 必须从相应 Workbench `Setup` 单元进入。
- 设置和求解完成后保存 Workbench 工程，使 `Setup` 和 `Solution` 显示绿色状态。
- 如果需要完整的 `Results` 绿色状态，则同时更新 Fluent 后处理或 CFD-Post。
- 外部 Case/Data 可以作为备份，但不能代替 Workbench 内部流程状态。

当前 V2 的实际计算结果是完整的，但 Workbench 封装不完整。后续修正时应建立一个新版本，例如：

`V3_50um_sJMC_Workbench_MultiCase`

其中共享同一套几何和网格，并把已有100 ml/min、600 W/cm²算例导入第一个 Fluent 分支，再建立其他热流和流量分支。

# ANSYS Fluent Microchannel

Use the user-required chain: **ANSYS Workbench → Geometry → ANSYS Meshing → Fluent**. Do not bypass ANSYS Meshing with Fluent Meshing unless the user explicitly changes this preference.

## Start with a simulation contract

Before solving, record:

- CAD path, ANSYS version, length unit, dimensionality, and whether the CAD contains both fluid and solid volumes.
- Which faces are `inlet`, `outlet`, `heat`, `symmetry`, and external `wall`.

- Whether the case is a literal reproduction, a geometry-adapted pilot, or a production case.

Never infer physical boundaries solely from screen position. A face is an inlet/outlet only when topology, flow direction, or user confirmation supports it.

## Workflow

2. Import the CAD through the Geometry cell. Confirm units and body count. Repair gaps, overlaps, slivers, non-manifold edges, and missing fluid volume before meshing.

---PREFERENCE---

# Workbench multi-case and Fluent launch preferences

For future ANSYS microchannel simulations, use one shared Geometry and ANSYS Meshing upstream in Workbench and branch the mesh into multiple independently named Fluent systems for different flow rates, heat fluxes, or other boundary-condition cases. The saved Workbench project must contain the Fluent setup and solution state, rather than only saving standalone `.cas.h5` and `.dat.h5` files outside Workbench. Launch Fluent in 3D double precision and use the highest effective local process count allowed by the installed license; the currently observed license permits 4-way parallel execution, so use 4 solver processes unless a later live check confirms a higher limit. Ensure Workbench Setup and Solution cells are updated/saved; update Results through Fluent/CFD-Post when a completed Results cell is required.

mesh1.SendCommand(

    Command="""
from System import Environment
from System.IO import File

report_path = 'C:/Users/PC/Documents/Codex/2026-09-11/x/outputs/wu2023_corrected_mesh_report.txt'
lines = []

def add_face_named_selection(name, ids):
    ns = ExtAPI.DataModel.Project.Model.AddNamedSelection()
    ns.Name = name
    sel = ExtAPI.SelectionManager.CreateSelectionInfo(SelectionTypeEnum.GeometryEntities)
    sel.Ids = ids
    ns.Location = sel
    lines.append('{0}={1}'.format(name, list(ids)))

try:
    # Vertical-flow top faces identified from Fig. 2 and the STEP face areas.
    add_face_named_selection('inlet', [603])
    add_face_named_selection('outlet', [630])
    add_face_named_selection('heated_bottom', [329])
    add_face_named_selection('symmetry_side_1', [230, 301, 331, 601, 628])
    add_face_named_selection('symmetry_side_2', [272, 306, 333, 599, 626])

    mesh = ExtAPI.DataModel.Project.Model.Mesh
    mesh.ElementSize = Quantity('0.005 [mm]')
    lines.append('global_element_size=0.005 mm')
    mesh.GenerateMesh()
    lines.append('mesh_generated=True')
    mesh_data = ExtAPI.DataModel.MeshDataByName('Global')
    lines.append('node_count={0}'.format(mesh_data.Nodes.Count))
    lines.append('element_count={0}'.format(mesh_data.Elements.Count))
except Exception as error:
    lines.append('error={0}'.format(error))

File.WriteAllText(report_path, Environment.NewLine.join(lines))
""",

    Language="Python")

mesh1.Exit()

Save(Overwrite=True)

2.9. Connecting Systems in Workbench (https://ansyshelp.ansys.com/public/Views/Secured/corp/v252/en/flu_wb/flu_wb_sec_wb_connect_systems.html)
citeturn17search0 [wordlim: 200] Published: last year; Crawled: last month; A shared data connection is represented in the Project Schematic by a line with a square on its right (target) side Image  (see Figure 2.4: Connected Systems Within Workbench). ... You can connect multiple Mesh cells of Mesh component systems to the Setup Cell of a Fluent-based system.

## 2.9. Connecting Systems in Workbench

In Workbench, you can create connections between multiple systems that enable the systems to access the same data. This is useful, for instance, when you want to compare the results from multiple Fluent-based systems in the same Ansys CFD-Post session. In this case, you would connect the various Solution cells to one Results cell (either in one of your Fluent-based systems or in a separate Results system). When you double-click that Results cell, the results from all connected systems will be loaded into Ansys CFD-Post at the same time.

Another example would be where you want to load multiple meshes created in the Ansys Meshing application to your Fluent-based system. See Connecting Multiple Upstream Meshes to a Setup Cell of a Fluent-Based System for further information on such connections.

Workbench supports two different types of connections:

  * Shared data connections

    * Used when the inputs and outputs of the two connected cells are identical

    * Can only be created between two cells of the same type

A shared data connection is represented in the Project Schematic by a line with a square on its right (target) side Image  (see Figure 2.4: Connected Systems Within Workbench).

  * Transfer data connections

    * Used when the output of one cell is used as the input to the connected cell

    * Usually created between two cells<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="7987" />.ansys.com/public/Views/Secured/corp/v252/en/wb2_js/wb2js_scriptingapps.html)

[64] assistant: 是的，你的判断正确。当前 Workbench 文件的 Fluent 流程确实没有完整封装，这是我之前保存方式的问题。

原因是：

- Workbench 中只完成并保存了 Geometry 和 ANSYS Meshing。
- Fluent 是通过外部自动化程序读取网格后独立求解的。
- 得到的 `.cas.h5` 和 `.dat.h5` 是有效且收敛的，但没有回写到 Workbench 的 Fluent 系统。
- 因此截图中：
  - `几何结构`、`网格`显示绿色对勾；
  - `设置 Setup` 显示问号；
  - `解决方案 Solution` 显示问号；
  - `结果 Results` 也没有更新。

这表示“Workbench不知道外部求解已经完成”，并不表示已有 Case/Data 没有收敛。

以后我将采用你截图中的方式：

```text
Geometry
   ↓
ANSYS Meshing
   ├── Fluent：F100_Q600
   ├── Fluent：F100_Q800
   ├── Fluent：F120_Q600
   └── Fluent：F120_Q800
```

同一个几何和网格只建立一次，不同流量、热流密度、入口温度或物性模型分别建立独立 Fluent 分支。每个分支都会使用清楚的工况名称。

同时执行以下规则：

- Fluent 使用3D双精度。
- 当前许可证只支持4核并行，因此默认使用4核。
- 如果以后检测到许可证支持更多核数，再提高进程数。
- 不直接照截图设置18核，因为超过许可证上限可能无法启动，或者被自动限制。
- Fluent 必须从相应 Workbench `Setup` 单元进入。
- 设置和求解完成后保存 Workbench 工程，使 `Setup` 和 `Solution` 显示绿色状态。
- 如果需要完整的 `Results` 绿色状态，则同时更新 Fluent 后处理或 CFD-Post。
- 外部 Case/Data 可以作为备份，但不能代替 Workbench 内部流程状态。

当前 V2 的实际计算结果是完整的，但 Workbench 封装不完整。后续修正时应建立一个新版本，例如：

`V3_50um_sJMC_Workbench_MultiCase`

其中共享同一套几何和网格，并把已有100 ml/min、600 W/cm²算例导入第一个 Fluent 分支，再建立其他热流和流量分支。

---
name: ansys-fluent-microchannel
description: "Build, inspect, reproduce, and audit ANSYS Workbench microchannel conjugate heat-transfer simulations using Geometry, ANSYS Meshing, and Fluent. Use when importing STEP/Parasolid CAD, creating mesh controls and Named Selections, mapping inlet/outlet/symmetry/heat boundaries, configuring laminar energy simulations, running convergence checks, or reproducing the BV18a4y117QQ manifold-microchannel method. Do not use for the unrelated Python FluidSim package."
---

# ANSYS Fluent Microchannel

Use the user-required chain: **ANSYS Workbench → Geometry → ANSYS Meshing → Fluent**. Do not bypass ANSYS Meshing with Fluent Meshing unless the user explicitly changes this preference.

## Start with a simulation contract

Before solving, record:

- CAD path, ANSYS version, length unit, dimensionality, and whether the CAD contains both fluid and solid volumes.
- Which faces are `inlet`, `outlet`, `heat`, `symmetry`, and external `wall`.
- Fluid and solid materials, inlet specification, inlet temperature, outlet pressure, heat input, and intended outputs.
- Whether the case is a literal reproduction, a geometry-adapted pilot, or a production case.

Never infer physical boundaries solely from screen position. A face is an inlet/outlet only when topology, flow direction, or user confirmation supports it.

## Workflow

1. Create or open a **Fluid Flow (Fluent)** system in Workbench.
2. Import the CAD through the Geometry cell. Confirm units and body count. Repair gaps, overlaps, slivers, non-manifold edges, and missing fluid volume before meshing.
3. Open the Mesh cell in ANSYS Meshing. Confirm the solver preference is Fluent.
4. Prefer sweep/mapped hex mesh where the topology permits it. Add local sizing across channel depth, fins/teeth, inlet/outlet transitions, and solid-fluid interfaces.
5. Create explicit Named Selections. Required minimum: `inlet`, `outle<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="795" />deo;
- values chosen for the user's adapted model.

Report project path, CAD linkage, boundary map, mesh metrics, solver settings, convergence evidence, and any unresolved physical assumption.


---PREFERENCE---

# Workbench multi-case and Fluent launch preferences

For future ANSYS microchannel simulations, use one shared Geometry and ANSYS Meshing upstream in Workbench and branch the mesh into multiple independently named Fluent systems for different flow rates, heat fluxes, or other boundary-condition cases. The saved Workbench project must contain the Fluent setup and solution state, rather than only saving standalone `.cas.h5` and `.dat.h5` files outside Workbench. Launch Fluent in 3D double precision and use the highest effective local process count allowed by the installed license; the currently observed license permits 4-way parallel execution, so use 4 solver processes unless a later live check confirms a higher limit. Ensure Workbench Setup and Solution cells are updated/saved; update Results through Fluent/CFD-Post when a completed Results cell is required.

# encoding: utf-8

# 2024 R2

SetScriptVersion(Version="24.2.133")

template1 = GetTemplate(TemplateName="Fluid Flow")

system1 = template1.CreateSystem()

geometry1 = system1.GetContainer(ComponentName="Geometry")

geometry1.SetFile(FilePath="C:/Users/PC/Desktop/micro channel/channel with manifold/yaner shang/origin jet/50juchi/danyaun/danyuan 50 juchi mmc.STEP")

geometryComponent1 = system1.GetComponent(Name="Geometry")

geometryComponent1.Refresh()

meshComponent1 = system1.GetComponent(Name="Mesh")

meshComponent1.Refresh()

Save(Overwrite=True)

mesh1 = system1.GetContainer(ComponentName="Mesh")

mesh1.Edit()

mesh1.SendCommand(

    Command="""
from System import Environment
from System.IO import File

report_path = 'C:/Users/PC/Documents/Codex/2026-09-11/x/outputs/wu2023_corrected_mesh_report.txt'
lines = []

def add_face_named_selection(name, ids):
    ns = ExtAPI.DataModel.Project.Model.AddNamedSelection()
    ns.Name = name
    sel = ExtAPI.SelectionManager.CreateSelectionInfo(SelectionTypeEnum.GeometryEntities)
    sel.Ids = ids
    ns.Location = sel
    lines.append('{0}={1}'.format(name, list(ids)))

try:
    # Vertical-flow top faces identified from Fig. 2 and the STEP face areas.
    add_face_named_selection('inlet', [603])
    add_face_named_selection('outlet', [630])
    add_face_named_selection('heated_bottom', [329])
    add_face_named_selection('symmetry_side_1', [230, 301, 331, 601, 628])
    add_face_named_selection('symmetry_side_2', [272, 306, 333, 599, 626])

    mesh = ExtAPI.DataModel.Project.Model.Mesh
    mesh.ElementSize = Quantity('0.005 [mm]')
    lines.append('global_element_size=0.005 mm')
    mesh.GenerateMesh()
    lines.append('mesh_generated=True')
    mesh_data = ExtAPI.DataModel.MeshDataByName('Global')
    lines.append('node_count={0}'.format(mesh_data.Nodes.Count))
    lines.append('element_count={0}'.format(mesh_data.Elements.Count))
except Exception as error:
    lines.append('error={0}'.format(error))

File.WriteAllText(report_path, Environment.NewLine.join(lines))
""",

    Language="Python")

mesh1.Exit()

Save(Overwrite=True)

2.9. Connecting Systems in Workbench (https://ansyshelp.ansys.com/public/Views/Secured/corp/v252/en/flu_wb/flu_wb_sec_wb_connect_systems.html)
citeturn17search0 [wordlim: 200] Published: last year; Crawled: last month; A shared data connection is represented in the Project Schematic by a line with a square on its right (target) side Image  (see Figure 2.4: Connected Systems Within Workbench). ... You can connect multiple Mesh cells of Mesh component systems to the Setup Cell of a Fluent-based system.

## 2.9. Connecting Systems in Workbench

In Workbench, you can create connections between multiple systems that enable the systems to access the same data. This is useful, for instance, when you want to compare the results from multiple Fluent-based systems in the same Ansys CFD-Post session. In this case, you would connect the various Solution cells to one Results cell (either in one of your Fluent-based systems or in a separate Results system). When you double-click that Results cell, the results from all connected systems will be loaded into Ansys CFD-Post at the same time.

Another example would be where you want to load multiple meshes created in the Ansys Meshing application to your Fluent-based system. See Connecting Multiple Upstream Meshes to a Setup Cell of a Fluent-Based System for further information on such connections.

Workbench supports two different types of connections:

  * Shared data connections

    * Used when the inputs and outputs of the two connected cells are identical

    * Can only be created between two cells of the same type

A shared data connection is represented in the Project Schematic by a line with a square on its right (target) side Image  (see Figure 2.4: Connected Systems Within Workbench).

  * Transfer data connections

    * Used when the output of one cell is used as the input to the connected cell

    * Usually created between two cells<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="7987" />.ansys.com/public/Views/Secured/corp/v252/en/wb2_js/wb2js_scriptingapps.html)

[62] assistant: 是的，你的判断正确。当前 Workbench 文件的 Fluent 流程确实没有完整封装，这是我之前保存方式的问题。

原因是：

- Workbench 中只完成并保存了 Geometry 和 ANSYS Meshing。
- Fluent 是通过外部自动化程序读取网格后独立求解的。
- 得到的 `.cas.h5` 和 `.dat.h5` 是有效且收敛的，但没有回写到 Workbench 的 Fluent 系统。
- 因此截图中：
  - `几何结构`、`网格`显示绿色对勾；
  - `设置 Setup` 显示问号；
  - `解决方案 Solution` 显示问号；
  - `结果 Results` 也没有更新。

这表示“Workbench不知道外部求解已经完成”，并不表示已有 Case/Data 没有收敛。

以后我将采用你截图中的方式：

```text
Geometry
   ↓
ANSYS Meshing
   ├── Fluent：F100_Q600
   ├── Fluent：F100_Q800
   ├── Fluent：F120_Q600
   └── Fluent：F120_Q800
```

同一个几何和网格只建立一次，不同流量、热流密度、入口温度或物性模型分别建立独立 Fluent 分支。每个分支都会使用清楚的工况名称。

同时执行以下规则：

- Fluent 使用3D双精度。
- 当前许可证只支持4核并行，因此默认使用4核。
- 如果以后检测到许可证支持更多核数，再提高进程数。
- 不直接照截图设置18核，因为超过许可证上限可能无法启动，或者被自动限制。
- Fluent 必须从相应 Workbench `Setup` 单元进入。
- 设置和求解完成后保存 Workbench 工程，使 `Setup` 和 `Solution` 显示绿色状态。
- 如果需要完整的 `Results` 绿色状态，则同时更新 Fluent 后处理或 CFD-Post。
- 外部 Case/Data 可以作为备份，但不能代替 Workbench 内部流程状态。

当前 V2 的实际计算结果是完整的，但 Workbench 封装不完整。后续修正时应建立一个新版本，例如：

`V3_50um_sJMC_Workbench_MultiCase`

其中共享同一套几何和网格，并把已有100 ml/min、600 W/cm²算例导入第一个 Fluent 分支，再建立其他热流和流量分支。

description: "Build, inspect, reproduce, and audit ANSYS Workbench microchannel conjugate heat-transfer simulations using Geometry, ANSYS Meshing, and Fluent. Use when importing STEP/Parasolid CAD, creating mesh controls and Named Selections, mapping inlet/outlet/symmetry/heat boundaries, configuring laminar energy simulations, running convergence checks, or reproducing the BV18a4y117QQ manifold-microchannel method. Do not use for the unrelated Python FluidSim package."

# ANSYS Fluent Microchannel

Use the user-required chain: **ANSYS Workbench → Geometry → ANSYS Meshing → Fluent**. Do not bypass ANSYS Meshing with Fluent Meshing unless the user explicitly changes this preference.

## Start with a simulation contract

Before solving, record:

- CAD path, ANSYS version, length unit, dimensionality, and whether the CAD contains both fluid and solid volumes.
- Which faces are `inlet`, `outlet`, `heat`, `symmetry`, and external `wall`.
- Fluid and solid materials, inlet specification, inlet temperature, outlet pressure, heat input, and intended outputs.
- Whether the case is a literal reproduction, a geometry-adapted pilot, or a production case.

Never infer physical boundaries solely from screen position. A face is an inlet/outlet only when topology, flow direction, or user confirmation supports it.

## Workflow

1. Create or open a **Fluid Flow (Fluent)** system in Workbench.
2. Import the CAD through the Geometry cell. Confirm units and body count. Repair gaps, overlaps, slivers, non-manifold edges, and missing fluid volume before meshing.
3. Open the Mesh cell in ANSYS Meshing. Confirm the solver preference is Fluent.
4. Prefer sweep/mapped hex mesh where the topology permits it. Add local sizing across channel depth, fins/teeth, inlet/outlet transitions, and solid-fluid interfaces.
5. Create explicit Named Selections. Required minimum: `inlet`, `outle<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="795" />deo;
- values chosen for the user's adapted model.

Report project path, CAD linkage, boundary map, mesh metrics, solver settings, convergence evidence, and any unresolved physical assumption.


---PREFERENCE---

# Workbench multi-case and Fluent launch preferences

For future ANSYS microchannel simulations, use one shared Geometry and ANSYS Meshing upstream in Workbench and branch the mesh into multiple independently named Fluent systems for different flow rates, heat fluxes, or other boundary-condition cases. The saved Workbench project must contain the Fluent setup and solution state, rather than only saving standalone `.cas.h5` and `.dat.h5` files outside Workbench. Launch Fluent in 3D double precision and use the highest effective local process count allowed by the installed license; the currently observed license permits 4-way parallel execution, so use 4 solver processes unless a later live check confirms a higher limit. Ensure Workbench Setup and Solution cells are updated/saved; update Results through Fluent/CFD-Post when a completed Results cell is required.

# encoding: utf-8

# 2024 R2

SetScriptVersion(Version="24.2.133")

template1 = GetTemplate(TemplateName="Fluid Flow")

system1 = template1.CreateSystem()

geometry1 = system1.GetContainer(ComponentName="Geometry")

geometry1.SetFile(FilePath="C:/Users/PC/Desktop/micro channel/channel with manifold/yaner shang/origin jet/50juchi/danyaun/danyuan 50 juchi mmc.STEP")

geometryComponent1 = system1.GetComponent(Name="Geometry")

geometryComponent1.Refresh()

meshComponent1 = system1.GetComponent(Name="Mesh")

meshComponent1.Refresh()

Save(Overwrite=True)

mesh1 = system1.GetContainer(ComponentName="Mesh")

mesh1.Edit()

mesh1.SendCommand(

    Command="""
from System import Environment
from System.IO import File

report_path = 'C:/Users/PC/Documents/Codex/2026-09-11/x/outputs/wu2023_corrected_mesh_report.txt'
lines = []

def add_face_named_selection(name, ids):
    ns = ExtAPI.DataModel.Project.Model.AddNamedSelection()
    ns.Name = name
    sel = ExtAPI.SelectionManager.CreateSelectionInfo(SelectionTypeEnum.GeometryEntities)
    sel.Ids = ids
    ns.Location = sel
    lines.append('{0}={1}'.format(name, list(ids)))

try:
    # Vertical-flow top faces identified from Fig. 2 and the STEP face areas.
    add_face_named_selection('inlet', [603])
    add_face_named_selection('outlet', [630])
    add_face_named_selection('heated_bottom', [329])
    add_face_named_selection('symmetry_side_1', [230, 301, 331, 601, 628])
    add_face_named_selection('symmetry_side_2', [272, 306, 333, 599, 626])

    mesh = ExtAPI.DataModel.Project.Model.Mesh
    mesh.ElementSize = Quantity('0.005 [mm]')
    lines.append('global_element_size=0.005 mm')
    mesh.GenerateMesh()
    lines.append('mesh_generated=True')
    mesh_data = ExtAPI.DataModel.MeshDataByName('Global')
    lines.append('node_count={0}'.format(mesh_data.Nodes.Count))
    lines.append('element_count={0}'.format(mesh_data.Elements.Count))
except Exception as error:
    lines.append('error={0}'.format(error))

File.WriteAllText(report_path, Environment.NewLine.join(lines))
""",

    Language="Python")

mesh1.Exit()

Save(Overwrite=True)

You can connect multiple Mesh cells of Mesh component systems to the Setup Cell of a Fluent-based system.

## 2.9. Connecting Systems in Workbench

In Workbench, you can create connections between multiple systems that enable the systems to access the same data. This is useful, for instance, when you want to compare the results from multiple Fluent-based systems in the same Ansys CFD-Post session. In this case, you would connect the various Solution cells to one Results cell (either in one of your Fluent-based systems or in a separate Results system). When you double-click that Results cell, the results from all connected systems will be loaded into Ansys CFD-Post at the same time.

Another example would be where you want to load multiple meshes created in the Ansys Meshing application to your Fluent-based system. See Connecting Multiple Upstream Meshes to a Setup Cell of a Fluent-Based System for further information on such connections.

Workbench supports two different types of connections:

  * Shared data connections

    * Used when the inputs and outputs of the two connected cells are identical

    * Can only be created between two cells of the same type

A shared data connection is represented in the Project Schematic by a line with a square on its right (target) side Image  (see Figure 2.4: Connected Systems Within Workbench).

  * Transfer data connections

    * Used when the output of one cell is used as the input to the connected cell

    * Usually created between two cells<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="7987" />.ansys.com/public/Views/Secured/corp/v252/en/wb2_js/wb2js_scriptingapps.html)

In Table 1: Scripting Support for Data-Integrated Applications, a Yes in the Supports Scripting with `SendCommand` column indicates that the data-integrated application can be driven from an Ansys Workbench script by manually inserting `SendCommand` calls.

[30] assistant: 我先核对三张截图与当前归档工程的实际状态。你看到的问号并不是 Fluent 没有算完，而是我此前把求解放在 Workbench 外部执行，导致计算结果没有回写到 Workbench 的 Setup/Solution 单元；这是工程封装不完整，我会把它作为后续流程必须修正的规则。



[35] assistant: 三点已经明确：以后每个几何/网格作为公共上游，一个 Workbench 工程内分支出多个独立 Fluent 工况；所有 Fluent 启动均开启双精度。核数不会盲设为18——当前许可证日志明确只允许4核并行，所以目前默认用4核，这是现有许可下的最高有效值。



[40] assistant: 是的，你的判断正确。当前 Workbench 文件的 Fluent 流程确实没有完整封装，这是我之前保存方式的问题。

原因是：

- Workbench 中只完成并保存了 Geometry 和 ANSYS Meshing。
- Fluent 是通过外部自动化程序读取网格后独立求解的。
- 得到的 `.cas.h5` 和 `.dat.h5` 是有效且收敛的，但没有回写到 Workbench 的 Fluent 系统。
- 因此截图中：
  - `几何结构`、`网格`显示绿色对勾；
  - `设置 Setup` 显示问号；
  - `解决方案 Solution` 显示问号；
  - `结果 Results` 也没有更新。

这表示“Workbench不知道外部求解已经完成”，并不表示已有 Case/Data 没有收敛。

以后我将采用你截图中的方式：

```text
Geometry
   ↓
ANSYS Meshing
   ├── Fluent：F100_Q600
   ├── Fluent：F100_Q800
   ├── Fluent：F120_Q600
   └── Fluent：F120_Q800
```

同一个几何和网格只建立一次，不同流量、热流密度、入口温度或物性模型分别建立独立 Fluent 分支。每个分支都会使用清楚的工况名称。

同时执行以下规则：

- Fluent 使用3D双精度。
- 当前许可证只支持4核并行，因此默认使用4核。
- 如果以后检测到许可证支持更多核数，再提高进程数。
- 不直接照截图设置18核，因为超过许可证上限可能无法启动，或者被自动限制。
- Fluent 必须从相应 Workbench `Setup` 单元进入。
- 设置和求解完成后保存 Workbench 工程，使 `Setup` 和 `Solution` 显示绿色状态。
- 如果需要完整的 `Results` 绿色状态，则同时更新 Fluent 后处理或 CFD-Post。
- 外部 Case/Data 可以作为备份，但不能代替 Workbench 内部流程状态。

当前 V2 的实际计算结果是完整的，但 Workbench 封装不完整。后续修正时应建立一个新版本，例如：

`V3_50um_sJMC_Workbench_MultiCase`

其中共享同一套几何和网格，并把已有100 ml/min、600 W/cm²算例导入第一个 Fluent 分支，再建立其他热流和流量分支。

citeturn18search12 [wordlim: 200] Published: 6 months ago; Ansys CFX in Ansys Workbench ... GetContainer(ComponentName="Setup") ... SendCommand(Command=r">gtmImport filename=C:\SYS-1.cmdb, \
Ansys CFX in Ansys Workbench

## Create the CFX system

```text
template1 = GetTemplate(TemplateName="CFX")
system1 = template1.CreateSystem(Position="Default")
```

## Edit the Setup cell and import a mesh (SYS-1.cmdb)

```text
setup1 = system1.GetContainer(ComponentName="Setup")
setup1.Edit()
setup1.SendCommand(Command=r">gtmImport filename=C:\SYS-1.cmdb, \
type=GTM_DSDB, genOpt= -names 'CFXMesh ACMO_Simulation' \
-contact read -relative 0.001, units=m, nameStrategy= Assembly")
```

Note:

The `setup1.SendCommand` command above must be entered as a single line; multiple lines are shown here for readability.

## Create an inlet boundary (in1)

```text
setup1.SendCommand(Command="""FLOW: Flow Analysis 1
  DOMAIN: Default Domain
&replace     BOUNDARY: in1
    Boundary Type = INLET
    Interface Boundary = Off
    Location = F18.12
    BOUNDARY CONDITIONS:
      FLOW REGIME:
        Option = Subsonic
      END # FLOW REGIME:
      MASS AND MOMENTUM:
        Normal Speed = 1 [m s^-1]
        Option = Normal Speed
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

L4478: ## TableInterpolation
L4479: 
L4480: An enum of the type of interpolation to use when accessing data from the table at a given index. The valid enum values are; None, Linear, Cubic Spline.
L4481: 
L4482: ### Possible Values
L4483: 
L4484: None  | Linear
L4485: No interpolation is done.  | A linear interpolation will be used to compute the return value.
L4486: 
L4487: * * *
L4488: 
L4489: ## TableInterpolationBeyondBounds
L4490: 
L4491: An enum which describes what will occur when the index is outside of the bounds of the table. The valid enum values are; Constant, Fit, Zero, and Error.
L4492: ### Possible Values
L4493: Constant  | Zero
L4494: his option will cause the value returned to be the data at the index nearest to the requested index.  | This option will cause the value returned to be zero if the index is outside of the bounds of the table.
L4495: Fit  | Error
L4496: This option will ignore the bounds of the table and use the Interpolation algorithm to fit the data and then compute the value at the requested index.  | If the data cannot be determined from the given index (outside of the bounds) then an error will occur.
L4497:

## CandidatesColoringMethods

Enumeration of the Coloring methods used for Candidates chart.

### Possible Values

ColoringPerPointType  | ColoringPerOutputNature
The color is used to distinguish the different types of candidate points (Starting Points, Candidate Points, etc).  | The color is used to distinguish the nature of the output values (Response Surface or Simulation).

* * *

## CappingSurfaceConstructionMethod

Options for choosing selection method for capping surface.

### Possible Values

CappingSurfaceFacesConstraint  | CappingSurfaceLoopsConstraint
Construct capping surface by capping all laminar loops of selected faces.  | Construct capping surface by capping all specified loops.
CappingSurfaceEdgesConstraint  | CappingSurfaceVerticesConstraint
Construct capping surface by capping all loops of selected edges.  | Construct capping surface by capping the loop formed by selected Vertices.

* * *

## CasePrecision

Precision of FLUENT Session.

### Possible Values

Single
Double

* * *

CCD_STANDARD_TEMPLATE  | CCD_ENHANCED_TEMPLATE
Standard  | Enhanced

# encoding: utf-8

# 2024 R2

SetScriptVersion(Version="24.2.133")

template1 = GetTemplate(TemplateName="Fluid Flow")

system1 = template1.CreateSystem()

geometry1 = system1.GetContainer(ComponentName="Geometry")

geometry1.SetFile(FilePath="C:/Users/PC/Desktop/micro channel/channel with manifold/yaner shang/origin jet/50juchi/danyaun/danyuan 50 juchi mmc.STEP")

geometryComponent1 = system1.GetComponent(Name="Geometry")

geometryComponent1.Refresh()

meshComponent1 = system1.GetComponent(Name="Mesh")

meshComponent1.Refresh()

Save(Overwrite=True)

mesh1 = system1.GetContainer(ComponentName="Mesh")

mesh1.Edit()

mesh1.SendCommand(

    Command="""

from System import Environment

from System.IO import File



report_path = 'C:/Users/PC/Documents/Codex/2026-09-11/x/outputs/wu2023_corrected_mesh_report.txt'

lines = []



def add_face_named_selection(name, ids):

    ns = ExtAPI.DataModel.Project.Model.AddNamedSelection()

    ns.Name = name

    sel = ExtAPI.SelectionManager.CreateSelectionInfo(SelectionTypeEnum.GeometryEntities)

    sel.Ids = ids

    ns.Location = sel

    lines.append('{0}={1}'.format(name, list(ids)))



try:

    # Vertical-flow top faces identified from Fig. 2 and the STEP face areas.

    add_face_named_selection('inlet', [603])

    add_face_named_selection('outlet', [630])

    add_face_named_selection('heated_bottom', [329])

    add_face_named_selection('symmetry_side_1', [230, 301, 331, 601, 628])

    add_face_named_selection('symmetry_side_2', [272, 306, 333, 599, 626])



    mesh = ExtAPI.DataModel.Project.Model.Mesh

    mesh.ElementSize = Quantity('0.005 [mm]')

    lines.append('global_element_size=0.005 mm')

    mesh.GenerateMesh()

    lines.append('mesh_generated=True')

    mesh_data = ExtAPI.DataModel.MeshDataByName('Global')

    lines.append('node_count={0}'.format(mesh_data.Nodes.Count))

    lines.append('element_count={0}'.format(mesh_data.Elements.Count))

except Exception as error:

    lines.append('error={0}'.format(error))



File.WriteAllText(report_path, Environment.NewLine.join(lines))

""",

    Language="Python")

mesh1.Exit()

Save(Overwrite=True)

L4478: ## TableInterpolation
L4479: 
L4480: An enum of the type of interpolation to use when accessing data from the table at a given index. The valid enum values are; None, Linear, Cubic Spline.
L4481: 
L4482: ### Possible Values
L4483: 
L4484: None  | Linear
L4485: No interpolation is done.  | A linear interpolation will be used to compute the return value.
L4486: 
L4487: * * *
L4488: 
L4489: ## TableInterpolationBeyondBounds
L4490: 
L4491: An enum which describes what will occur when the index is outside of the bounds of the table. The valid enum values are; Constant, Fit, Zero, and Error.
L4492: ### Possible Values
L4493: Constant  | Zero
L4494: his option will cause the value returned to be the data at the index nearest to the requested index.  | This option will cause the value returned to be zero if the index is outside of the bounds of the table.
L4495: Fit  | Error
L4496: This option will ignore the bounds of the table and use the Interpolation algorithm to fit the data and then compute the value at the requested index.  | If the data cannot be determined from the given index (outside of the bounds) then an error will occur.
L4497: 
L4498: * * *
L4499: ## TessellationRefinement
L4500: 
L4501: Used to specify value to be used for tessellation refinement. The default value is AutomaticallyDetermined.
L4502: 
L4503: ### Possible Values
L4504: 
L4505: AutomaticallyDetermined  | Sets tessellation refinement to user defined value.
L4506: Sets tessellation refinement to 10% of the value of Min Size/Proximity Min Size.  | Off
L4507: UserDefined  | Tessellation refinement is not performed.
L4508: 
L4509: * * *

## CandidatesColoringMethods

Enumeration of the Coloring methods used for Candidates chart.

### Possible Values

ColoringPerPointType  | ColoringPerOutputNature
The color is used to distinguish the different types of candidate points (Starting Points, Candidate Points, etc).  | The color is used to distinguish the nature of the output values (Response Surface or Simulation).

* * *

## CappingSurfaceConstructionMethod

Options for choosing selection method for capping surface.

### Possible Values

CappingSurfaceFacesConstraint  | CappingSurfaceLoopsConstraint
Construct capping surface by capping all laminar loops of selected faces.  | Construct capping surface by capping all specified loops.
CappingSurfaceEdgesConstraint  | CappingSurfaceVerticesConstraint
Construct capping surface by capping all loops of selected edges.  | Construct capping surface by capping the loop formed by selected Vertices.

* * *

## CasePrecision

Precision of FLUENT Session.

### Possible Values

Single
Double

* * *

## CCDTemplateType

Enumeration for the Template Type for CCD algorithm.

### Possible Values

CCD_STANDARD_TEMPLATE  | CCD_ENHANCED_TEMPLATE
Standard  | Enhanced

* * *

Enumeration of the available design types for the Central Composite Design algorithm.

CCDTYPE_FACE_CENT  | VIF-Optimality
Face-Centered  | CCDTYPE_G_OPT
CCDTYPE_ROT  | G-Optimal<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="7744" />Systems

Used to specify whether mesh may deviate from geometry as a result of wrapper surface improvement. The default value is MeetTarget.

MeetTarget  | MaintainGeometry

L26: ### Edit

L35:

L39:

L47: ### Import

# encoding: utf-8

# 2024 R2

SetScriptVersion(Version="24.2.133")

template1 = GetTemplate(TemplateName="Fluid Flow")

system1 = template1.CreateSystem()

geometry1 = system1.GetContainer(ComponentName="Geometry")

geometry1.SetFile(FilePath="C:/Users/PC/Desktop/micro channel/channel with manifold/yaner shang/origin jet/50juchi/danyaun/danyuan 50 juchi mmc.STEP")

geometryComponent1 = system1.GetComponent(Name="Geometry")

geometryComponent1.Refresh()

meshComponent1 = system1.GetComponent(Name="Mesh")

meshComponent1.Refresh()

Save(Overwrite=True)

mesh1 = system1.GetContainer(ComponentName="Mesh")

mesh1.Edit()

mesh1.SendCommand(

    Command="""

from System import Environment

from System.IO import File



report_path = 'C:/Users/PC/Documents/Codex/2026-09-11/x/outputs/wu2023_corrected_mesh_report.txt'

lines = []



def add_face_named_selection(name, ids):

    ns = ExtAPI.DataModel.Project.Model.AddNamedSelection()

    ns.Name = name

    sel = ExtAPI.SelectionManager.CreateSelectionInfo(SelectionTypeEnum.GeometryEntities)

    sel.Ids = ids

    ns.Location = sel

    lines.append('{0}={1}'.format(name, list(ids)))



try:

    # Vertical-flow top faces identified from Fig. 2 and the STEP face areas.

    add_face_named_selection('inlet', [603])

    add_face_named_selection('outlet', [630])

    add_face_named_selection('heated_bottom', [329])

    add_face_named_selection('symmetry_side_1', [230, 301, 331, 601, 628])

    add_face_named_selection('symmetry_side_2', [272, 306, 333, 599, 626])



    mesh = ExtAPI.DataModel.Project.Model.Mesh

    mesh.ElementSize = Quantity('0.005 [mm]')

    lines.append('global_element_size=0.005 mm')

    mesh.GenerateMesh()

    lines.append('mesh_generated=True')

    mesh_data = ExtAPI.DataModel.MeshDataByName('Global')

    lines.append('node_count={0}'.format(mesh_data.Nodes.Count))

    lines.append('element_count={0}'.format(mesh_data.Elements.Count))

except Exception as error:

    lines.append('error={0}'.format(error))



File.WriteAllText(report_path, Environment.NewLine.join(lines))

""",

    Language="Python")

mesh1.Exit()

Save(Overwrite=True)

L4478: ## TableInterpolation
L4479: 
L4480: An enum of the type of interpolation to use when accessing data from the table at a given index. The valid enum values are; None, Linear, Cubic Spline.
L4481: 
L4482: ### Possible Values
L4483: 
L4484: None  | Linear
L4485: No interpolation is done.  | A linear interpolation will be used to compute the return value.
L4486: 
L4487: * * *
L4488: 
L4489: ## TableInterpolationBeyondBounds
L4490: 
L4491: An enum which describes what will occur when the index is outside of the bounds of the table. The valid enum values are; Constant, Fit, Zero, and Error.
L4492: ### Possible Values
L4493: Constant  | Zero
L4494: his option will cause the value returned to be the data at the index nearest to the requested index.  | This option will cause the value returned to be zero if the index is outside of the bounds of the table.
L4495: Fit  | Error
L4496: This option will ignore the bounds of the table and use the Interpolation algorithm to fit the data and then compute the value at the requested index.  | If the data cannot be determined from the given index (outside of the bounds) then an error will occur.
L4497: 
L4498: * * *
L4499: ## TessellationRefinement
L4500: 
L4501: Used to specify value to be used for tessellation refinement. The default value is AutomaticallyDetermined.
L4502: 
L4503: ### Possible Values
L4504: 
L4505: AutomaticallyDetermined  | Sets tessellation refinement to user defined value.
L4506: Sets tessellation refinement to 10% of the value of Min Size/Proximity Min Size.  | Off
L4507: UserDefined  | Tessellation refinement is not performed.
L4508: 
L4509: * * *
L4510:

## CandidatesColoringMethods

Enumeration of the Coloring methods used for Candidates chart.

### Possible Values

ColoringPerPointType  | ColoringPerOutputNature
The color is used to distinguish the different types of candidate points (Starting Points, Candidate Points, etc).  | The color is used to distinguish the nature of the output values (Response Surface or Simulation).

* * *

## CappingSurfaceConstructionMethod

Options for choosing selection method for capping surface.

### Possible Values

CappingSurfaceFacesConstraint  | CappingSurfaceLoopsConstraint
Construct capping surface by capping all laminar loops of selected faces.  | Construct capping surface by capping all specified loops.
CappingSurfaceEdgesConstraint  | CappingSurfaceVerticesConstraint
Construct capping surface by capping all loops of selected edges.  | Construct capping surface by capping the loop formed by selected Vertices.

* * *

## CasePrecision

Precision of FLUENT Session.

### Possible Values

Single
Double

* * *

## CCDTemplateType

Enumeration for the Template Type for CCD algorithm.

### Possible Values

CCD_STANDARD_TEMPLATE  | CCD_ENHANCED_TEMPLATE
Standard  | Enhanced

* * *

### Data Types

Data Types
A breakdown of the types represented in this document

## ActionMode

Enumeration with the available ROM action modes.

### Possible Values

None  | Production mode, when a snapshot file is generated each time a design point is updated.
No action.  | Consumption
Production  | Consumption mode, when a ROM project is used to evaluate the results.

* * *

## AdaptKrigOutType

Enumeration for the Output Variable Combinations type when refining for the Kriging algorithm.

### Possible Values

AK_MAXOUT  | All Outputs
Maximum Output  | AK_SUMOUT
AK_ALLOUT  | Sum of Outputs

* * *

## Aggressive

Used to specify whether mesh may deviate from geometry as a result of wrapper surface improvement. The default value is MeetTarget.

### Possible Values

MeetTarget  | MaintainGeometry
Modify the geometry as needed to meet quality target.  | Compromise targets to maintain geometry.

* * *

## AlgorithmType

Available contact detection algorithm types.

### Possible Values

Unknown
ProximityBased
Static  | Topology optimization analysis
Computation of the system that is time independent.  | EigenvalueBuckling
TimeDependent  | Eigenvalue buckling
Computation of the system that is time dependent.  | All
Modal  | All domains.

* * *

## CandidatesColoringMethods

Enumeration of the Coloring methods used for Candidates chart.

### Possible Values

ColoringPerPointType  | ColoringPerOutput<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="7538" />eature offered by the operating system as these can sometimes update to an older version of the driver.

L14: ### FLUENT Setup
L15: 
L16: FLUENT Setup
L17: This container holds Setup data for an instance of FLUENT.
L18: 
L19: ## Methods
L20: 
L21: ### CopyLauncherSettings
L22: 
L23: Copies the FLUENT Launcher Settings from one FLUENT Setup or Solution container to another FLUENT Setup or Solution container.
L24: 
L25: * * *
L26: ### Edit
L27: 
L28: Opens the FLUENT editor to allow modification of FLUENT data.
L29: 
L30: This command will open the editor only if one is not already open on this system. If this system’s editor is already open and in interactive mode, then it will be raised to the front.
L31: 
L32: * * *
L33: 
L34: ### Exit
L35: 
L36: Exits the FLUENT editor.
L37: 
L38: If no editor is open on the component in question, this command will have no effect.
L39: 
L40: * * *
L41: ### GetFluentLauncherSettings
L42: 
L43: Returns the Data Entity which contains the Setup container's settings for the FLUENT Launcher.
L44: 
L45: * * *
L46: 
L47: ### Import
L48: 
L49: Imports the FLUENT mesh and FLUENT case settings into the FLUENT editor from an existing FLUENT .msh or .cas file; replacing the current FLUENT mesh and FLUENT case settings. If the imported file contains only a mesh, then the FLUENT case settings will be set to FLUENT's default values.

# encoding: utf-8

# 2024 R2

SetScriptVersion(Version="24.2.133")

template1 = GetTemplate(TemplateName="Fluid Flow")

system1 = template1.CreateSystem()

geometry1 = system1.GetContainer(ComponentName="Geometry")

geometry1.SetFile(FilePath="C:/Users/PC/Desktop/micro channel/channel with manifold/yaner shang/origin jet/50juchi/danyaun/danyuan 50 juchi mmc.STEP")

geometryComponent1 = system1.GetComponent(Name="Geometry")

geometryComponent1.Refresh()

meshComponent1 = system1.GetComponent(Name="Mesh")

meshComponent1.Refresh()

Save(Overwrite=True)

mesh1 = system1.GetContainer(ComponentName="Mesh")

mesh1.Edit()

mesh1.SendCommand(

    Command="""

from System import Environment

from System.IO import File



report_path = 'C:/Users/PC/Documents/Codex/2026-09-11/x/outputs/wu2023_corrected_mesh_report.txt'

lines = []



def add_face_named_selection(name, ids):

    ns = ExtAPI.DataModel.Project.Model.AddNamedSelection()

    ns.Name = name

    sel = ExtAPI.SelectionManager.CreateSelectionInfo(SelectionTypeEnum.GeometryEntities)

    sel.Ids = ids

    ns.Location = sel

    lines.append('{0}={1}'.format(name, list(ids)))



try:

    # Vertical-flow top faces identified from Fig. 2 and the STEP face areas.

    add_face_named_selection('inlet', [603])

    add_face_named_selection('outlet', [630])

    add_face_named_selection('heated_bottom', [329])

    add_face_named_selection('symmetry_side_1', [230, 301, 331, 601, 628])

    add_face_named_selection('symmetry_side_2', [272, 306, 333, 599, 626])



    mesh = ExtAPI.DataModel.Project.Model.Mesh

    mesh.ElementSize = Quantity('0.005 [mm]')

    lines.append('global_element_size=0.005 mm')

    mesh.GenerateMesh()

    lines.append('mesh_generated=True')

    mesh_data = ExtAPI.DataModel.MeshDataByName('Global')

    lines.append('node_count={0}'.format(mesh_data.Nodes.Count))

    lines.append('element_count={0}'.format(mesh_data.Elements.Count))

except Exception as error:

    lines.append('error={0}'.format(error))



File.WriteAllText(report_path, Environment.NewLine.join(lines))

""",

    Language="Python")

mesh1.Exit()

Save(Overwrite=True)

## CandidatesColoringMethods

Enumeration of the Coloring methods used for Candidates chart.

### Possible Values

ColoringPerPointType  | ColoringPerOutputNature
The color is used to distinguish the different types of candidate points (Starting Points, Candidate Points, etc).  | The color is used to distinguish the nature of the output values (Response Surface or Simulation).

## CappingSurfaceConstructionMethod

Options for choosing selection method for capping surface.

### Possible Values

CappingSurfaceFacesConstraint  | CappingSurfaceLoopsConstraint
Construct capping surface by capping all laminar loops of selected faces.  | Construct capping surface by capping all specified loops.
CappingSurfaceEdgesConstraint  | CappingSurfaceVerticesConstraint
Construct capping surface by capping all loops of selected edges.  | Construct capping surface by capping the loop formed by selected Vertices.

mesh1.SendCommand(

    Command="""

from System import Environment

from System.IO import File



report_path = 'C:/Users/PC/Documents/Codex/2026-09-11/x/outputs/wu2023_corrected_mesh_report.txt'

lines = []



def add_face_named_selection(name, ids):

    ns = ExtAPI.DataModel.Project.Model.AddNamedSelection()

    ns.Name = name

    sel = ExtAPI.SelectionManager.CreateSelectionInfo(SelectionTypeEnum.GeometryEntities)

    sel.Ids = ids

    ns.Location = sel

    lines.append('{0}={1}'.format(name, list(ids)))



try:

    # Vertical-flow top faces identified from Fig. 2 and the STEP face areas.

    add_face_named_selection('inlet', [603])

    add_face_named_selection('outlet', [630])

    add_face_named_selection('heated_bottom', [329])

    add_face_named_selection('symmetry_side_1', [230, 301, 331, 601, 628])

    add_face_named_selection('symmetry_side_2', [272, 306, 333, 599, 626])



    mesh = ExtAPI.DataModel.Project.Model.Mesh

    mesh.ElementSize = Quantity('0.005 [mm]')

    lines.append('global_element_size=0.005 mm')

    mesh.GenerateMesh()

    lines.append('mesh_generated=True')

    mesh_data = ExtAPI.DataModel.MeshDataByName('Global')

    lines.append('node_count={0}'.format(mesh_data.Nodes.Count))

    lines.append('element_count={0}'.format(mesh_data.Elements.Count))

except Exception as error:

    lines.append('error={0}'.format(error))



File.WriteAllText(report_path, Environment.NewLine.join(lines))

""",

    Language="Python")

mesh1.Exit()

Save(Overwrite=True)

## CandidatesColoringMethods

Enumeration of the Coloring methods used for Candidates chart.

### Possible Values

ColoringPerPointType  | ColoringPerOutputNature
The color is used to distinguish the different types of candidate points (Starting Points, Candidate Points, etc).  | The color is used to distinguish the nature of the output values (Response Surface or Simulation).

* * *

## CappingSurfaceConstructionMethod

Options for choosing selection method for capping surface.

### Possible Values

CappingSurfaceFacesConstraint  | CappingSurfaceLoopsConstraint
Construct capping surface by capping all laminar loops of selected faces.  | Construct capping surface by capping all specified loops.
CappingSurfaceEdgesConstraint  | CappingSurfaceVerticesConstraint
Construct capping surface by capping all loops of selected edges.  | Construct capping surface by capping the loop formed by selected Vertices.

def add_face_named_selection(name, ids):

    ns = ExtAPI.DataModel.Project.Model.AddNamedSelection()

    ns.Name = name

    sel = ExtAPI.SelectionManager.CreateSelectionInfo(SelectionTypeEnum.GeometryEntities)

    sel.Ids = ids

    ns.Location = sel

    mesh = ExtAPI.DataModel.Project.Model.Mesh

    mesh.ElementSize = Quantity('0.005 [mm]')

    lines.append('global_element_size=0.005 mm')

    mesh.GenerateMesh()

    lines.append('mesh_generated=True')

    mesh_data = ExtAPI.DataModel.MeshDataByName('Global')

    lines.append('element_count={0}'.format(mesh_data.Elements.Count))

except Exception as error:

    lines.append('error={0}'.format(error))



File.WriteAllText(report_path, Environment.NewLine.join(lines))

""",

    Language="Python")

mesh1.Exit()

# encoding: utf-8

# 2024 R2

SetScriptVersion(Version="24.2.133")

template1 = GetTemplate(TemplateName="Fluid Flow")

system1 = template1.CreateSystem()

geometry1 = system1.GetContainer(ComponentName="Geometry")

geometry1.SetFile(FilePath="C:/Users/PC/Desktop/micro channel/channel with manifold/yaner shang/origin jet/50juchi/danyaun/danyuan 50 juchi mmc.STEP")

geometryComponent1 = system1.GetComponent(Name="Geometry")

geometryComponent1.Refresh()

meshComponent1 = system1.GetComponent(Name="Mesh")

meshComponent1.Refresh()

Save(Overwrite=True)

mesh1 = system1.GetContainer(ComponentName="Mesh")

mesh1.Edit()

mesh1.SendCommand(

    Command="""

from System import Environment

from System.IO import File



report_path = 'C:/Users/PC/Documents/Codex/2026-09-11/x/outputs/wu2023_corrected_mesh_report.txt'

lines = []



def add_face_named_selection(name, ids):

    ns = ExtAPI.DataModel.Project.Model.AddNamedSelection()

    ns.Name = name

    sel = ExtAPI.SelectionManager.CreateSelectionInfo(SelectionTypeEnum.GeometryEntities)

    sel.Ids = ids

    ns.Location = sel

    lines.append('{0}={1}'.format(name, list(ids)))



try:

    # Vertical-flow top faces identified from Fig. 2 and the STEP face areas.

    add_face_named_selection('inlet', [603])

    add_face_named_selection('outlet', [630])

    add_face_named_selection('heated_bottom', [329])

    add_face_named_selection('symmetry_side_1', [230, 301, 331, 601, 628])

    add_face_named_selection('symmetry_side_2', [272, 306, 333, 599, 626])



    mesh = ExtAPI.DataModel.Project.Model.Mesh

    mesh.ElementSize = Quantity('0.005 [mm]')

    lines.append('global_element_size=0.005 mm')

    mesh.GenerateMesh()

    lines.append('mesh_generated=True')

    mesh_data = ExtAPI.DataModel.MeshDataByName('Global')

    lines.append('node_count={0}'.format(mesh_data.Nodes.Count))

    lines.append('element_count={0}'.format(mesh_data.Elements.Count))

except Exception as error:

    lines.append('error={0}'.format(error))



File.WriteAllText(report_path, Environment.NewLine.join(lines))

""",

    Language="Python")

mesh1.Exit()

Save(Overwrite=True)

def add_face_named_selection(name, ids):

    ns = ExtAPI.DataModel.Project.Model.AddNamedSelection()

    ns.Name = name

    sel = ExtAPI.SelectionManager.CreateSelectionInfo(SelectionTypeEnum.GeometryEntities)

#### Command Line Execution of Ansys Workbench

Ansys Workbench can be executed from the operating system command line and accepts a number of command line arguments to facilitate automation and the replay of scripts. The following command can be used to run Ansys Workbench from the command line:
    
              `ANSYS_INSTALL_PATH`/v252/Framework/bin/`PLATFORM`/runwb2

Where `PLATFORM` is one of the following (as applicable to the application or tool that you are using):

`Win64`
`Linux64`

For example, to run Ansys Workbench from the default installation location on a Windows 64-bit system, the command would be:
    
    C:\Program Files\ANSYS Inc\V252\Framework\bin\win64\runwb2

The following table describes the command line arguments that can be used to control Ansys Workbench file operations and execution behavior at start-up.

Argument  | Operation
--- | ---
`-B`  | Run Ansys Workbench in batch mode. In this mode, the user interface is not displayed and a console window is opened. The functionality of the console window is the same as the Ansys Workbench command window.
`-R` ` WorkbenchScriptFile`  | Replay the specified Ansys Workbench script file on start-up. If specified in conjunction with `–B`, Ansys Workbench will start in batch mode, execute the specified script, and shut do<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="7801" />es%”. ... Editing a Job Definition

## Boolean

A Boolean is a data type that has one of two possible values (usually True/False or On/Off).

Optionally specify a default value, list of possible values (for string, float, or integer types), and description for the property.

<visual_element id="e1">
A job-definition row showing the property “Read START.ANS file at start-up”, type set to “Boolean” with a checked checkbox, and a description field reading “Reads the START.ANS file when session starts”; a trash/delete icon appears at the far right.
</visual_element>

## Execution

In the **Execution** area of a task definition you can specify whether the evaluator will execute the task using a command or script as well as conditions under which an evaluator should terminate the task.

### Execute By

Choose either **Command** or **Script**.

- If **Command** is selected, enter either the Windows command or Linux shell command to be executed. There are special placeholders or variables, which are defined using a % syntax, that pass information to the command. Some important placeholders are:

**%executable%**

Replaced with the path to the application's executable, like **AnsysFW.exe** or **runwb2** in the case of Workbench on Windows or Linux, respectively.

**%file:name_of_the_file%**

Replaced with paths to input or output files that are defined in the **Files (p. 71)** area.

If needed, you can also access these placeholders in the command:

- %memory%
- %num_cores%
- %disk_space%
- %platform%

Here is a typical command for executing Mechanical APDL (MAPDL):

<visual_element id="e2">
A command entry row labeled “Command” with a text box containing: “%executable% -b -i %file:mac% -o file.out -np %num_cores%”.
</visual_element>
